game name
= 遊戲名稱

Only games found here
= 僅顯示已找到的遊戲

Show only the games whose saves were found on this computer. Typing a name searches every game.
= 僅顯示在這臺電腦上找到存檔的遊戲。輸入名稱會搜尋所有遊戲。

Any support
= 全部支援程度

Close
= 關閉

Pick a game to see where it keeps its saves and the ones on this computer. Open… and dragging a file onto the window still work for anything else.
= 選擇遊戲即可檢視它的存檔位置以及這臺電腦上的存檔。其他情況仍可使用「開啟…」或將檔案拖到視窗中。

On this computer
= 這臺電腦上

Console games
= 主機遊戲

Looking for saves on this computer…
= 正在這臺電腦上查詢存檔…

No game matches. Use Open… to pick the file yourself.
= 沒有匹配的遊戲。請用「開啟…」自行選擇檔案。

Looking for saves…
= 正在查詢存檔…

If the game keeps them somewhere else (another drive, a cloud folder), use Open… to pick the file.
= 如果遊戲把存檔放在其他位置（其他磁碟、雲資料夾），請用「開啟…」選擇檔案。

= ‹ 全部遊戲

= 正在以十六進位顯示解碼後的資料。儲存時會重新應用原始編碼。

File
= 檔案

Open…
= 開啟…

Find my game's saves…
= 查詢我的遊戲存檔…

Pick a game to see the saves it has on this computer
= 選擇遊戲即可檢視它在這臺電腦上的存檔

Open recent
= 最近開啟

Nothing yet
= 暫無

Save
= 儲存

Save a copy as…
= 另存副本為…

Reload from disk
= 從磁碟重新載入

Reads the file again, for when the game or another program changed it. Unsaved changes are dropped (you are asked first).
= 重新讀取檔案，適用於遊戲或其他程式修改了檔案的情況。未儲存的更改會被丟棄（會先詢問）。

Close save
= 關閉存檔

Close this save and go back to the start page (Ctrl+W)
= 關閉此存檔並返回起始頁 (Ctrl+W)

Close the open save and go back to the start page
= 關閉目前存檔並返回起始頁

Revert all changes
= 還原所有更改

Export decoded data…
= 匯出解碼資料…

Import edited data…
= 匯入編輯後的資料…

Bring back data you exported and edited in another program; it is packed the way this save is packed when you save.
= 匯入你匯出並在其他程式中編輯過的資料；儲存時會按此存檔的方式重新打包。

Backups
= 備份

Back up now…
= 立即備份…

Keeps a named backup of the save file as it is on disk now, until you delete it. The save itself is not changed.
= 將磁碟上目前的存檔保留為一個命名備份，直到你刪除它。存檔本身不會改變。

Back up and start a new game…
= 備份並開始新遊戲…

Keeps a named backup, then removes the save file so the game starts a new game. Restore the backup any time to go back.
= 保留一個命名備份，然後刪除存檔檔案，讓遊戲開始新遊戲。隨時可恢復備份回到原來的進度。

Save manager…
= 存檔管理器…

All backups of this save in one window: restore, compare, delete, name. Restoring backs up the current save first.
= 在一個視窗中檢視此存檔的所有備份：恢復、比較、刪除、命名。恢復前會先備份目前存檔。

Quick restore
= 快速恢復

Automatic backups are taken each time this app saves over the file.
= 每次本應用覆蓋儲存檔案時，都會自動建立備份。

Compare
= 比較

Edit
= 編輯

Undo
= 撤銷

= 重做

Find…
= 查詢…

Copy changes as text
= 將更改複製為文字

The list of changes, to paste when asking for help or sharing what you did
= 變更清單，可在求助或分享操作時貼上。

Preferences…
= 偏好設定…

= 檢視

= 顯示原始鍵名

Changes panel
= 更改面板

Review changes before saving
= 儲存前檢查更改

Lists what changed and checks whether the game is running each time you save.
= 每次儲存時列出更改內容，並檢查遊戲是否正在執行。

= 縮放：Ctrl + / Ctrl − / Ctrl 0

= 以十六進位顯示解碼資料

Tools
= 工具

Locate game data folder (RPG Maker names)…
= 定位遊戲資料資料夾（RPG Maker 名稱）…

Only for RPG Maker saves.
= 僅適用於 RPG Maker 存檔。

Decrypt with password…
= 使用密碼解密…

Open a save first.
= 請先開啟存檔。

For encrypted saves (Easy Save 3, Godot). Easy Save's default password is tried automatically.
= 用於加密存檔（Easy Save 3、Godot）。會自動嘗試 Easy Save 的預設密碼。

Change Steam account in this save…
= 更改此存檔中的 Steam 帳號…

Finds the Steam IDs stored in the save and changes them to another account's, so a save copied from a friend or another account loads.
= 查詢存檔中儲存的 Steam ID 並改為另一個帳號的，使從朋友或其他帳號複製來的存檔能夠載入。

Cheat patches…
= 作弊補丁…

Only for saves opened in the hex view, which means a format the editor doesn't understand yet.
= 僅適用於以十六進位檢視開啟的存檔，即編輯器尚不支援的格式。

Ready-made cheats for console saves (PlayStation 1–4, PSP, Vita, Dreamcast) from the Apollo patch collection.
= 來自 Apollo 補丁集的主機存檔（PlayStation 1–4、PSP、Vita、Dreamcast）現成作弊碼。

Find checksum…
= 查詢總和檢查碼…

Looks for a checksum in a save the editor doesn't know (CRC, Adler, sums, MD5, SHA) and keeps it right whenever you save.
= 在編輯器不認識的存檔中查詢總和檢查碼（CRC、Adler、求和、MD5、SHA），並在每次儲存時保持其正確。

Templates folder…
= 模板資料夾…

Your own templates for games the editor doesn't know: a JSON file per game that says where each value is. The folder has a guide with an example.
= 為編輯器不認識的遊戲準備的自定義模板：每個遊戲一個 JSON 檔案，說明每個數值所在位置。資料夾中有帶示例的指南。

Presets
= 預設

Save changes as a preset…
= 將更改儲存為預設…

Keep the changes you made, to make them again on another save or slot
= 儲存你所做的更改，以便在其他存檔或存檔位上重做

Apply a preset…
= 應用預設…

Make a saved set of changes on this save
= 將已儲存的一組更改應用到此存檔

Compare with another save…
= 與另一個存檔比較…

Open another file in this archive
= 開啟此壓縮包中的其他檔案

Save or revert your changes first.
= 請先儲存或還原更改。

Those two files aren't the same kind of save, so they can't be compared.
= 這兩個檔案不是同一類存檔，無法比較。

Values that differ between this save and the other one. “Use theirs” copies the other save's value into this one.
= 此存檔與另一個存檔中不同的數值。「使用對方的」會把另一個存檔的數值複製到此存檔。

Use theirs
= 使用對方的

Back up and start a new game
= 備份並開始新遊戲

The save is backed up under this name and then removed, so the game starts a new game. Restore the backup later to come back to it.
= 存檔會以此名稱備份，然後被刪除，讓遊戲開始新遊戲。之後恢復該備份即可回到原來的進度。

for example: first run
= 例如：首輪遊戲

for example: before the final boss
= 例如：最終 Boss 之前

Save manager
= 存檔管理器

There is no save file here right now. The game makes a new one when you save in it, or restore a backup below to put one back.
= 這裡目前沒有存檔檔案。在遊戲中儲存時會生成新的，或在下方恢復備份來放回一個。

Keep backups in another folder…
= 將備份儲存到其他資料夾…

Back to the default folder
= 恢復預設資料夾

backup name, e.g. first run
= 備份名稱，例如 首輪遊戲

Back up this save
= 備份此存檔

Saves a named backup, then removes the current save file so the game starts a new game. Restore the backup later to go back.
= 儲存一個命名備份，然後刪除目前存檔檔案，讓遊戲開始新遊戲。之後恢復該備份即可回到原來的進度。

For games with a single save: use “Back up and start a new game”, play a new run, and restore the old backup later.
= 對於只有單個存檔的遊戲：使用「備份並開始新遊戲」，開一局新的，之後再恢復舊備份。

No backups yet. One is made automatically each time you save, or press “Back up this save”.
= 還沒有備份。每次儲存時會自動建立一個，也可以點「備份此存檔」。

Restore this backup
= 恢復此備份

Replaces the current save with this backup. The current save is backed up first, so you can undo it.
= 用此備份替換目前存檔。目前存檔會先被備份，因此可以撤銷。

Shows what differs between this backup and the open save
= 顯示此備份與已開啟存檔的差異

Make automatic
= 設為自動

Stops keeping this backup for good; it is cleared out in time like the automatic ones
= 不再永久保留此備份；它會像自動備份一樣，過一段時間後被清理

Delete backup
= 刪除備份

Permanently removes this backup
= 永久刪除此備份

That backup is automatic now: it is cleared out in time like the others.
= 該備份現在是自動備份：會像其他備份一樣，過一段時間後被清理。

Deleted the backup.
= 已刪除備份。

That file has no cheat codes in it.
= 該檔案中沒有作弊碼。

Cheat patches
= 作弊補丁

Game:
= 遊戲：

name or title ID (SLUS-20312, BLES00371…)
= 名稱或標題 ID（SLUS-20312、BLES00371…）

Open patch file…
= 開啟補丁檔案…

No patches were found from this save's file or folder name. Type the game's name or title ID.
= 根據此存檔的檔名或資料夾名沒有找到補丁。請輸入遊戲名稱或標題 ID。

This save was unpacked with the patch file's own codes when it opened; they run again when you save.
= 此存檔在開啟時已用補丁檔案自帶的程式碼解包；儲存時這些程式碼會再次執行。

Show codes for other files too
= 同時顯示其他檔案的程式碼

This one is a Python script, which only Apollo itself can run.
= 這是一個 Python 腳本，只有 Apollo 本身才能執行。

Big-endian (PS3)
= 大端序 (PS3)

Byte order for numbers in the codes. Set from the patch file's console; change it only if you know the save uses the other one.
= 程式碼中數字的位元組序。根據補丁檔案所屬主機自動設定；僅當你確定存檔使用另一種位元組序時才更改。

Back up first: a code meant for another version of the game can damage the save. Codes change the data now; save to write the file.
= 請先備份：用於其他版本遊戲的程式碼可能損壞存檔。程式碼會立即修改資料；儲存後才會寫入檔案。

The codes didn't change anything (the values may already be set).
= 這些程式碼沒有改變任何內容（數值可能已經設定好了）。

No Steam ID was found in this save's data (some games key the save to the account in other ways).
= 在此存檔的資料中未找到 Steam ID（有些遊戲以其他方式將存檔與帳號關聯）。

Change Steam account in this save
= 更改此存檔中的 Steam 帳號

Steam IDs in this save:
= 此存檔中的 Steam ID：

Change it to:
= 改為：

17-digit Steam ID or account number
= 17 位 Steam ID 或帳號編號

Accounts on this computer:
= 這臺電腦上的帳號：

Every copy of the ID changes; save to write the file. Some games also check the folder the save sits in.
= 每處 ID 都會被更改；儲存後才會寫入檔案。有些遊戲還會檢查存檔所在的資料夾。

Change
= 更改

Find checksum
= 查詢總和檢查碼

No checksum the editor knows was found at the start or end of this file.
= 在此檔案的開頭或末尾沒有找到編輯器能識別的總和檢查碼。

It may be encrypted, compressed, or checked with something the game made up. The value search still works on it.
= 它可能已加密、壓縮，或使用了遊戲自創的校驗方式。數值搜尋仍然可用。

Pick one to have it written again every time you save. Saving a few times and searching again narrows a long list.
= 選擇一個，每次儲存時都會重新寫入。多儲存幾次再搜尋，可縮小長清單範圍。

(kept fixed)
= （保持固定）

Keep fixed
= 保持固定

Stop fixing the checksum
= 停止固定總和檢查碼

The checksum will be written again every time you save this file.
= 每次儲存此檔案時，總和檢查碼都會被重新寫入。

Decrypt with password
= 使用密碼解密

Some games encrypt their saves with a password built into the game.
= 有些遊戲使用內建在遊戲中的密碼加密存檔。

Supported: Easy Save 3 (Unity) and Godot encrypted files. Easy Save's default password is tried automatically.
= 支援：Easy Save 3 (Unity) 和 Godot 加密檔案。會自動嘗試 Easy Save 的預設密碼。

password
= 密碼

Decrypt
= 解密

That password didn't decrypt the file (or the encryption isn't one this editor knows).
= 該密碼無法解密此檔案（或者這種加密方式本編輯器不支援）。

= 從清單中選擇遊戲，即可檢視它在這臺電腦上的存檔

Ctrl+Z
= Ctrl+Z

Ctrl+Y
= Ctrl+Y

Ren'Py 8.1+ signs its saves. Your Ren'Py key was found on this PC, so edited saves are re-signed and load normally.
= Ren'Py 8.1+ 會對存檔簽名。已在這臺電腦上找到你的 Ren'Py 金鑰，因此編輯後的存檔會被重新簽名並正常載入。

The game was running when this file was opened. Quit it before saving, or it may overwrite your changes.
= 開啟此檔案時遊戲正在執行。請在儲存前退出遊戲，否則它可能覆蓋你的更改。

Point to the game's data folder to see variable, switch and item names

Whole document (Raw JSON edit)
= 整個文件 (Raw JSON 編輯)

Show in list
= 在清單中顯示

Cairn saves in this slot
= 此存檔位中的 Cairn 存檔

This save's run
= 此存檔的這一局

As on the game's slot card. The card's own copy of the play time updates the next time the game saves.
= 與遊戲存檔位卡片上的顯示一致。卡片自帶的遊玩時長會在遊戲下次儲存時更新。

Named values
= 已命名的數值

Binary saves have no names. Find a value with Value search, click it, then name it here — names are remembered for this file.
= 二進位存檔沒有名稱。用「數值搜尋」找到數值並點選它，然後在此命名——名稱會針對此檔案記住。

Set
= 設定

Write this value (or press Enter)
= 寫入此數值（或按 Enter）

Forget this name (the value is not changed)
= 忘記此名稱（數值不變）

Add
= 新增

Value search
= 數值搜尋

Don't know the number (a health bar, a hidden counter)? Start with “Unknown starting value”, then refine with Increased, Decreased or Changed after each in-game save.
= 不知道具體數字（比如血條、隱藏計數器）？先選「未知初始值」，然後每次遊戲內儲存後，用「增加」「減少」或「已變化」來篩選。

Know the value? Search for it, change it in-game, save, and refine with the new value. Don't know it (a health bar, a hidden counter)? Start with “Unknown starting value”, then refine with Increased, Decreased or Changed after each in-game save.
= 知道數值？先搜尋它，在遊戲中修改並儲存，再用新數值篩選。不知道（比如血條、隱藏計數器）？先選「未知初始值」，然後每次遊戲內儲存後，用「增加」「減少」或「已變化」來篩選。

to
= 至

Refine
= 篩選

Re-reads the file from disk (save in-game first) and keeps only the locations that pass the test above
= 從磁碟重新讀取檔案（請先在遊戲內儲存），只保留透過上方條件的位置

Aligned
= 對齊

Only test offsets that are a multiple of the value size. Faster, and how most games store numbers.
= 僅測試值大小整數倍的偏移量。更快，也符合大多數遊戲儲存數字的方式。

Refine by itself each time the game saves
= 遊戲每次儲存時自動篩選

Watches the file and refines with the test above as soon as the game writes it again
= 監視檔案，遊戲一再次寫入就立即按上方條件篩選

Every location is still in play.
= 所有位置仍在候選之中。

Big-endian
= 大端序

Most PC games use little-endian (unticked)
= 大多數 PC 遊戲使用小端序（不勾選）

Go to
= 轉到

hex offset
= 十六進位偏移量

Go
= 轉到

Click to edit as this type
= 點選以此型別編輯

new value
= 新數值

Write
= 寫入

Pick your game from the list; its saves on this computer are found for you
= 從清單中選擇你的遊戲，會自動為你找到它在這臺電腦上的存檔

or drag a file anywhere onto this window
= 或將檔案拖到此視窗的任意位置

Recent files
= 最近的檔案

Files you open will appear here.
= 你開啟的檔案會顯示在這裡。

Restore a backup…
= 恢復備份…

The file isn't there any more, but it has backups
= 檔案已不存在，但有備份

Find your saves
= 查詢你的存檔

RPG Maker games usually keep saves in a “save” folder next to the game.
= RPG Maker 遊戲的存檔通常位於遊戲旁邊的「save」資料夾中。

What it can open
= 可開啟的內容

Dismiss
= 忽略

(empty file)
= （空檔案）

Unsaved changes
= 未儲存的更改

You have unsaved changes. Save them before closing this file?
= 有未儲存的更改。關閉此檔案前是否儲存？

Save and close
= 儲存並關閉

Close without saving
= 不儲存並關閉

You have unsaved changes. Save them before closing?
= 有未儲存的更改。關閉前是否儲存？

That save was not found at {0}. It was moved or deleted, or its drive is not connected.
= 在 {0} 找不到該存檔。它可能已被移動或刪除，或其所在驅動器未連線。

= 重新搜尋驅動器

Website
= 網站

Report a problem…
= 報告問題…

Opens a form on GitHub. The version and system are filled in; your save is not sent.
= 在 GitHub 上開啟表單。版本和系統資訊已自動填寫；不會傳送你的存檔。

Request a game…
= 請求新增遊戲…

Opens a form on GitHub
= 在 GitHub 上開啟表單

Questions and ideas…
= 問題與建議…

Opens the discussions on GitHub, to ask a question or suggest an idea
= 開啟 GitHub 上的討論區，提問或提出建議

Licences and credits
= 許可與致謝

On a Mac, use Cmd instead of Ctrl.
= 在 Mac 上請用 Cmd 代替 Ctrl。

It builds on save-format research, tables and code from many other projects, each listed with its author and licence in the third-party notices.
= 它基於許多其他專案的存檔格式研究、資料表和程式碼，每一項的作者和許可證都列在第三方宣告中。

The interface uses egui and eframe (MIT or Apache-2.0).
= 介面使用 egui 和 eframe（MIT 或 Apache-2.0）。

Edit game saves safely. Everything stays on your device: nothing is uploaded, and every save is backed up before it is written.
= 安全地編輯遊戲存檔。所有內容都保留在你的裝置上，不會上傳任何東西，每次寫入存檔前都會先備份。

In the browser, Japanese, Chinese and Korean names may show as boxes. The downloadable app uses your computer's fonts for them.
= 在瀏覽器中，日文、中文和韓文名稱可能顯示為方框。可下載的應用會使用你電腦上的字型來顯示它們。

Preferences
= 偏好設定

Look
= 外觀

Language:
= 語言：

Same as the system
= 跟隨系統

Menus, buttons and messages. Game screens and value names stay as the game has them.
= 適用於選單、按鈕和訊息。遊戲畫面和數值名稱保持遊戲原樣。

Theme:
= 主題：

= 深色

Light
= 淺色

= 縮放：

= 重置

= 在十六進位檢視中顯示查詢數值指南

Saving
= 儲存

Show the list of changes, and warn when the game is running, before writing the save
= 寫入存檔前顯示更改清單，並在遊戲執行時發出警告

Automatic backups to keep per save:
= 每個存檔保留的自動備份數：

A backup is taken every time this app writes a save. Named backups are always kept.
= 本應用每次寫入存檔時都會建立備份。命名備份始終保留。

Backups go:
= 備份位置：

Choose a folder…
= 選擇資料夾…

Back to the default
= 恢復預設

Finding saves
= 查詢存檔

Also search removable and network drives
= 同時搜尋可移動驅動器和網路驅動器

Saves are always looked for on every fixed drive. USB sticks and network drives are skipped unless this is on, because they can be slow. Use Search again after changing it.
= 始終會在所有固定驅動器上查詢存檔。除非開啟此選項，否則會跳過隨身碟和網路驅動器，因為它們可能很慢。更改後請使用「重新搜尋」。

# src/app/safety.rs

Keep what's shown
= 保留目前顯示的內容

Hide this. Saving still asks before writing over the newer file.
= 隱藏此提示。儲存時若要覆蓋較新的檔案，仍會先詢問。

The save changed on disk
= 磁碟上的存檔已更改

Saving now would put the version you opened, with your changes, over that newer file.
= 現在儲存會用你開啟的版本（含你的更改）覆蓋那個較新的檔案。

Either way, whatever is on disk now is backed up before anything is written.
= 無論選哪種，寫入之前都會先備份磁碟上現有的內容。

Reload the newer file
= 重新載入較新的檔案

Open what's on disk now. Your unsaved changes are dropped.
= 開啟磁碟上現有的內容。未儲存的更改會被丟棄。

Save anyway
= 仍然儲存

Not saved. Your changes are still here.
= 未儲存。你的更改仍然保留。

A PlayStation 2 save (memory card, .psu or .psv) of the USA, Europe, Japanese or Final Mix release. Munny, difficulty, magic levels, each party member's level, experience, HP, MP and bonuses, and the stock of every item, weapon, armor, accessory, key item and synthesis material. The save's checksum is written again.

A PlayStation 2 save (memory card, .psu or .psv) of the original game or Final Mix. Munny, difficulty, magic levels, the ten party members' level, experience, HP, MP, AP, strength, defense and weapon, and the stock of every item, weapon, accessory, key item and synthesis material.
= PlayStation 2 存檔（儲存卡、.psu 或 .psv），適用於原版和 Final Mix。蒙尼（Munny）、難度、魔法等級，十位隊員的等級、經驗值、HP、MP、AP、力量、防禦和武器，以及每種物品、武器、飾品、關鍵物品和合成材料的庫存。

Close the emulator first. Gold, play time and the other numbers are limited to what the game can show; the save's memory-card preview (name, gold and time) is kept in step.
= 請先關閉模擬器。金幣、遊戲時間等數值受限於遊戲能顯示的範圍；儲存卡中存檔的預覽（名稱、金幣和時間）會同步更新。

Close the emulator first. Levels follow experience (1000 per level for the party, 500 for guardian forces). The save's CRC-16 is written again; the story progression is left to you, since a wrong value can break the story.
= 請先關閉模擬器。等級跟隨經驗值（隊伍每級 1000，守護神每級 500）。存檔的 CRC-16 會重新寫入；劇情進度交由你自己決定，因為錯誤的值可能會破壞劇情。

Close the emulator first. Level, experience, Brave, Faith and equipment are edited as the game stores them (experience and level are separate bytes: set both). The save's checksum is written again.
= 請先關閉模擬器。等級、經驗值、Brave、Faith 和裝備按遊戲的儲存方式編輯（經驗值和等級是分開的位元組：請兩者都設定）。存檔的總和檢查碼會重新寫入。

Close the emulator first. Health is capped at 140, the game's own maximum; the quantity is the ammunition or the number of uses.
= 請先關閉模擬器。生命值上限為 140（遊戲本身的最大值）；數量為彈藥數或使用次數。

Close the emulator first. The save's checksum is written again. USA, Europe, Japanese and Final Mix saves are read (the Final Mix only items show in Final Mix saves); the stock holds at most 99 of each item.
= 請先關閉模擬器。存檔的總和檢查碼會重新寫入。支援讀取美版、歐版、日版和 Final Mix 版存檔（Final Mix 專屬物品只在 Final Mix 存檔中顯示）；每種物品庫存最多 99。

Close the emulator first. The level reached is the number of the level you can play next (the game counts finished levels from it). The gem and key counts, the completion rate in the save's title and the checksum are written again.
= 請先關閉模擬器。到達的關卡是你接下來可以玩的關卡編號（遊戲據此計算已通關關卡數）。寶石與鑰匙的數量、存檔標題中的完成度和總和檢查碼會重新寫入。

Close the emulator first. A stock number is how many you own (99 at most; key items 0 or 1). The Final Mix only items (synthesis materials) are listed for every release but only matter in Final Mix.
= 請先關閉模擬器。庫存數字表示你擁有的數量（最多 99；關鍵物品為 0 或 1）。Final Mix 專屬物品（合成材料）在所有版本中都會列出，但只在 Final Mix 中有效。

Close the emulator first. Times are in seconds; 0 means the mission is not done and 1023 means it was skipped.
= 請先關閉模擬器。用時以秒為單位；0 表示任務未完成，1023 表示已跳過。

Close the emulator first. Times are in seconds. The game keeps its own totals of jiggies and notes in the flags, so change the flags and the counts together.
= 請先關閉模擬器。用時以秒為單位。遊戲在標誌中也儲存拼圖塊和音符的總數，所以請同時修改標誌和數量。

Close the emulator first. Health is in sixteenths of a heart. Each edited file's sum is made again and its backup copy is updated.
= 請先關閉模擬器。生命值以心的 1/16 為單位。每個被編輯存檔的總和檢查碼會重新計算，其備份副本也會更新。

Close the emulator first. Times are in 1/60 s (3600 is one minute).
= 請先關閉模擬器。用時以 1/60 秒為單位（3600 為一分鐘）。

<name>_<date>.yls in the Saves folder (a SQLite database, written back as one). The first row of each table is yours; the other youtuber and channel rows are the game's rivals and friends.
= <name>_<date>.yls，位於 Saves 資料夾（SQLite 資料庫，會按資料庫寫回）。每個表的第一行是你自己的，其餘 YouTuber 和頻道行是遊戲中的對手和朋友。

CurrentPlayerSave.sav: the pilot's permanent progress. DNA fragments buy perks in the Lab; every perk shows its level and whether it is still locked. Credits, resources and the ship of a run are in the run's own save files.
= CurrentPlayerSave.sav：飛行員的永久進度。DNA 碎片可在實驗室購買特長，每個特長顯示其等級和是否仍被鎖定。一局遊戲的信用點、資源和飛船在該局自己的存檔檔案中。

LBAL.save (the run in progress). The save is one JSON object per line; each node of the run is shown under its path. Symbols and items are lists of names in the Tree.
= LBAL.save（進行中的一局）。存檔每行一個 JSON 物件；這一局的每個節點都顯示在其路徑下。符號和物品是樹中的名稱清單。

SaveFile-<day>-<month>-<year>-<time>.save. Money is counted in copper (100 copper = 1 silver, 100 silver = 1 gold). Items are shown by their game id.
= SaveFile-<day>-<month>-<year>-<time>.save。金錢以銅幣計算（100 銅幣 = 1 銀幣，100 銀幣 = 1 金幣）。物品按遊戲內 ID 顯示。

gameN.sav is a list of 1,000 values, one per line (stored as base64 text); the screen names the lines worked out by the Luna Nights save editor. Abilities and keys are owned when the value equals their line number (80 to 84, 90 to 94) and 0 when not.
= gameN.sav 是包含 1000 個值的清單，每行一個（以 base64 文字儲存）；介面會標出 Luna Nights 存檔編輯器所推斷的行。當數值等於其行號（80 至 84、90 至 94）時表示擁有該能力或鑰匙，否則為 0。

r_profile<id>.json in the QuickSave folder. Stardust is the meta currency; the unlocked items live in r_items.json, which the game encrypts (not opened). Mastery and records are in the r_stats file.
= r_profile<id>.json，位於 QuickSave 資料夾。星塵是元貨幣；已解鎖物品在 r_items.json 中，遊戲對其加密（未開啟）。精通和記錄在 r_stats 檔案中。

r_stats<id>.json in the QuickSave folder: mastery and the record of every traveler.
= r_stats<id>.json，位於 QuickSave 資料夾：每位旅行者的精通和記錄。

save-<name>-<number>.json. Meat is the money; muscle, mysticality, moxie and every skill and perk are in the skills list below (skills are levels, perks are 1 or 0). The game stores most numbers as text, so type digits only.
= save-<name>-<number>.json。肉就是金錢；肌肉、神秘、膽量以及所有技能和特長都在下方的技能清單中（技能是等級，特長是 1 或 0）。遊戲把大多數數字存成文字，所以只能輸入數字。

Play time, Adol's and Laxia's equipment and every item you hold, by item id (up to 999 each). Both checksums are updated when you save.
= 遊戲時間、阿道和拉克西的裝備以及你持有的所有物品（按物品 ID，每種最多 999）。儲存時會更新兩個總和檢查碼。

Each party member's level, HP, MP, stats and experience, and the item counts in every bag (up to the item's own maximum). The CRC is updated when you save.
= 每位隊員的等級、HP、MP、屬性和經驗，以及每個背包中各物品的數量（最多為該物品自身的上限）。儲存時會更新 CRC。

Gold, gems, facility and weapon materials, each officer's attack, defence and health, and the weapon-compatibility stars. The checksums are updated when you save.
= 金幣、寶石、設施和武器材料，每位武將的攻擊、防禦和生命，以及武器適性星級。儲存時會更新總和檢查碼。

Each character's health, attack, defence, special bars and skill slots (a level-up may reset the stats). The checksum is updated when you save.
= 每個角色的生命、攻擊、防禦、特殊槽和技能槽（升級可能會重置屬性）。儲存時會更新總和檢查碼。

Beli and the quantity of every coin you already own (by coin id). The object checksums are updated when you save.
= 貝里以及你已擁有的每種硬幣的數量（按硬幣 ID）。儲存時會更新物件總和檢查碼。

= 每個存檔位：金錢、人格面具點數、羈絆點數，以及你已持有的消耗品、烹飪食材、香、狀態藥和技能卡的數量。儲存時會更新總和檢查碼。

<number>.save in the Ubisoft Connect savegames folder (game 1875 or 1957). The save is unscrambled, unpacked and packed again with its block checks; the account it belongs to is kept. Close the game and turn cloud saves off in Ubisoft Connect first, or the old save may come back. The save ends with a 4-byte value that isn't understood yet and is kept as it is; edited saves of this game have not been tried in the game.

A .GM1, .CGM or .GM2 save from the game's Games folder (Shadow of Death, Armageddon's Blade and Horn of the Abyss 1.8+). Each player's resources, and each hero's level, experience, attributes, spell points, movement, army and the skills they already have.
= 遊戲Games資料夾中的.GM1、.CGM或.GM2存檔（Shadow of Death、Armageddon's Blade和Horn of the Abyss 1.8+）。每位玩家的資源，以及每位英雄的等級、經驗、屬性、法術點、移動力、軍隊和已有技能。

A .prison save. Only the bank balance and the top-level settings are shown; the rest of the file is left byte for byte as it was.
= .prison存檔。僅顯示銀行餘額和頂層設定；檔案其餘部分逐位元組保持原樣。

A 1CHARACTERSLOT file from Last Epoch's Saves folder (offline characters; the EPOCH header is kept). Level, experience, deaths and respecs. Items, passives and the monolith are in the Tree.
= Last Epoch的Saves資料夾中的1CHARACTERSLOT檔案（離線角色；保留EPOCH頭）。等級、經驗、死亡次數和洗點次數。物品、被動技能和巨石在樹中。

= Quicksave 或 Autosave 檔案。金錢是背包中的 Coin 道具；技能等級和四個科技點池在下方。

= SAVEn.sav（.NET BinaryFormatter 檔案，會自動解包並寫回）。名稱、金幣、經驗、遊戲時間、隊伍成員，以及道具、武器和護甲的 id。結構參考自 BenjaminUrquhart 的 DeltatravelerSaveEditor。

E

E = equipped (not swapped here). Consumables can be swapped for other consumables and gear for other gear; the first empty place takes any item.
= E=已裝備(此處不可替換)。消耗品可換為其他消耗品,裝備可換為其他裝備;第一個空位可放任何物品。

EP
= EP

ES2_<date>.sav in the game's SaveGames folder (the packed Unreal save is unpacked for you; big saves take a few seconds to open). Ships, items and missions are in the Tree tab.
= 遊戲 SaveGames 資料夾中的 ES2_<date>.sav(打包的 Unreal 存檔會自動解包;大型存檔需數秒才能開啟)。飛船、物品和任務在「樹」標籤頁中。

Each currency has a most the game allows; a larger amount may be cut when it loads. Currencies past pixels and ancient essence come from mods.
= 每種貨幣都有遊戲允許的上限;超出的數額在載入時可能被截斷。畫素和遠古精華之後的貨幣來自模組。

Each item has its own largest stack in the game (1000 for most materials); the game may cut a bigger count when it loads.
= 每種物品在遊戲中都有各自的堆疊上限(大多數材料為 1000);超出的數量在載入時可能被截斷。

Each item's level (I–X). Items without a name are upgrades and ammo, shown by the game's item number.
= 每件物品的等級(I–X)。無名稱的物品為升級件和彈藥,以遊戲物品編號顯示。

Each line is a stack of coins in this character's inventory (named by its stats entry).
= 每行是該角色背包中的一堆硬幣(以其屬性條目命名)。

Each line is one stack of coins in this character's inventory.
= 每行是該角色背包中的一堆硬幣。

Each mastery's level and the points in each skill. Names are the game's internal records (mastery folder: file name).
= 各精通的等級和每項技能的點數。名稱為遊戲內部記錄名(精通資料夾:檔名)。

Each part's health. The game keeps the most a part can have in its race data, not in the save, so it isn't shown here; healthy humans have the same value on every part.
= 各部位的生命值。部位上限儲存在遊戲的種族資料中而非存檔中,因此此處不顯示;健康人類每個部位的數值相同。

Each piece keeps its place, its links and what it's equipped to; only its level changes. Levels another piece of yours already has aren't offered.
= 每個部件保留其位置、連線和裝備物件,僅等級改變。您的其他部件已有的等級不會提供。

Each runs 0–100 on normal settings. Low nutrition shrinks the most health you can have.
= 在常規設定下各項範圍為 0–100。營養不足會降低最大生命值。

Each stack of these the colony has on the map, with its position. A stack can't hold more than the game's stack limit for that item, so for more, raise several stacks.
= 殖民地在地圖上擁有的每一堆此類物品及其位置。每堆不能超過遊戲對該物品的堆疊上限,如需更多請提高多堆數量。

Each upgrade level is its own entry.
= 每個升級等級都是獨立條目。

Each weapon you own and the level it has been upgraded to.
= 您擁有的每件武器及其強化等級。

Experience (total)

Experience in hand
= 持有經驗

Experience is shared by the party; raising it gives level-ups to pick in the game.
= 經驗值由隊伍共享;提高經驗值會產生可在遊戲中選擇的升級。

Experience is what Kiryu has earned and not yet spent in the Abilities menu (99,999,999 at most). Money tops out at ¥9,999,999,999.
= 經驗值是桐生已獲得但尚未在能力選單中花費的部分(最多 99,999,999)。金錢上限為 ¥9,999,999,999。

Experience is what a character has earned since the last rest; the game banks it, and raises the level, when the party rests at a camp or hotel. Levels, HP, MP and stats are shown as saved.
= 經驗值是角色自上次休息以來獲得的部分；隊伍在營地或旅館休息時，遊戲會將其入帳並提升等級。等級、HP、MP 和屬性依存檔值顯示。

Experience sets the level the game works out; the level shown is from the save's header and catches up when you next gain experience. Karma runs from −1000 to 1000.
= 經驗值決定遊戲計算出的等級;顯示的等級來自存檔頭部,下次獲得經驗時會更新。善惡值範圍為 −1000 到 1000。

Items and currencies

Item names come from the game's data; an item the editor doesn't know yet shows as Item and its number. 999 is the most the editor lets you set.
= 物品名稱來自遊戲資料;編輯器尚不認識的物品顯示為 Item 加編號。編輯器允許設定的最大值為 999。

Items are listed by their ID because the game's item names aren't in the save. Everything else is in the Values tab.
= 由於遊戲的物品名稱不在存檔中,物品按 ID 列出。其餘內容都在「數值」標籤頁。

Items are shown by their prefab name. Each item has its own top stack size and quality in the game; going past them may be undone when it loads.
= 物品按預製件名稱顯示。遊戲中每種物品有自己的堆疊上限和品質;超出後載入時可能被還原。

Items are shown by their template name. Changing money, levels or XP updates the save only; the save list's summary updates the next time the game saves.
= 物品按模板名稱顯示。修改金錢、等級或經驗只會更新存檔;存檔清單中的摘要會在遊戲下次存檔時更新。

= 物品按遊戲資源名稱顯示。可修改堆疊數(箭、藥水)和充能(法杖);兩者都沒有的物品顯示 –。此處無法新增物品。

Raises Bismor, Magnite, Croppa, Umanite, Jadiz, Enor Pearl, Phazyonite and the four brewing ingredients you already have. Credits, Data Cells, Error Cubes and Blank Cores are left alone.
= 提升已擁有的 Bismor、Magnite、Croppa、Umanite、Jadiz、Enor Pearl、Phazyonite 以及四種釀造原料。積分、Data Cells、Error Cubes 和 Blank Cores 不受影響。

Raises every crafting and upgrade material you already have to 999, the game's stack limit. Food, seafood, ammunition and the valuables are left alone.
= 將已擁有的所有制作和升級材料提升到 999（遊戲的堆疊上限）。食物、海鮮、彈藥和貴重物品不受影響。

Raises every fish and farm ingredient you already have, the stock the sushi bar cooks from. Gold, Bei and gear are left alone.
= 提升已擁有的所有魚類和農場食材，即壽司店烹飪所用的庫存。金幣、Bei 和裝備不受影響。

Raises every weapon element you already own to 999, the stack limit in the official manual (Ultimate allows 9,999; 999 is safe for both). Elements you don't own are left alone.
= 將已擁有的所有武器元素提升到 999（官方手冊中的堆疊上限；Ultimate 允許 9,999，999 對兩者都安全）。未擁有的元素不受影響。

Raises logs, sticks, rocks, bones, cloth, circuit boards, wire, duct tape, rope, feathers, leaves, small rocks, tech mesh, Solafite ore and printer resin you already carry to the game's own carrying limit for each. Weapons, ammo, food, blueprints and story items are left alone.
= 將已攜帶的原木、木棍、石頭、骨頭、布料、電路板、電線、強力膠帶、繩索、羽毛、樹葉、小石子、科技網、Solafite 礦石和印表機樹脂提升到遊戲對各自的攜帶上限。武器、彈藥、食物、藍圖和劇情物品不受影響。

Raises lumber, stone, bones, grass, wooden planks, stone blocks, gold nuggets, gold bars, crystal shards, spider silk and shells you already have. Coins, food, seeds, gifts, necklaces, boss items and key items are left alone.
= 提升已擁有的木材、石頭、骨頭、草、木板、石塊、金塊、金條、水晶碎片、蜘蛛絲和貝殼。硬幣、食物、種子、禮物、項鍊、Boss 物品和關鍵物品不受影響。

Raises metal parts, synthetic material, biomaterial, superconductors, chemicals, energy cells and microelectronics you already hold to 999 (an estimate: no public source states the stack limit, and saves with 437 are known). Neural modules are left alone.
= 將已持有的金屬部件、合成材料、生物材料、超導體、化學品、能量電池和微電子元件提升到 999（估計值：沒有公開來源說明堆疊上限，已知有 437 的存檔）。神經模組不受影響。

Raises rocks, sticks, skulls, rope, bones, skins, rabbit fur and circuit boards to the limit the game stores for each in the save. Items with no limit (cloth, leaves, feathers, teeth, sap, tape), tools, weapons, food and story items are left alone.
= 將石頭、木棍、頭骨、繩索、骨頭、獸皮、兔毛和電路板提升到遊戲在存檔中為各自儲存的上限。無上限的物品（布料、樹葉、羽毛、牙齒、樹液、膠帶）、工具、武器、食物和劇情物品不受影響。

Raises scrap and weapon parts to 999 (an estimate: no public source states a cap). Medpacks, artifacts, notes and gear are left alone.
= 將廢料和武器部件提升到 999（估計值：沒有公開來源說明上限）。醫療包、神器、筆記和裝備不受影響。

Raises the Moonstones for normal and special weapons and Legion Calibers you already have. Quest items, Quartz and consumables are left alone.
= 提升已擁有的普通武器和特殊武器月石以及軍團口徑。任務物品、石英和消耗品不受影響。

Raises the Tarforge smithing materials (Ventrium, Laterite, Dorsalite, Thoracium and Thoracium Prime) you already have. Keys, artifacts, tools and upgrade items are left alone.
= 提升已擁有的 Tarforge 鍛造材料（Ventrium、Laterite、Dorsalite、Thoracium 和 Thoracium Prime）。鑰匙、神器、工具和升級物品不受影響。

Raises the consumable stacks you already carry to 99. Key items are left alone.
= 將已攜帶的消耗品堆疊提升到 99。關鍵物品不受影響。

Raises the count of each item you already hold to 99.
= 將已擁有的每種物品數量提升到 99。

Raises the ores, crystals, scrap metal and basic crafting resources you already have to 99,999 (the game states no cap, so this is an estimate). Catalysts, components, rift and faction resources and story items are left alone.
= 將已擁有的礦石、水晶、廢金屬和基礎製作資源提升到 99,999（遊戲未說明上限，因此為估計值）。催化劑、元件、裂隙與陣營資源和劇情物品不受影響。

Raises the plain building and crafting materials in the bag (wood, stone, clay, leather, straw, salt, fur, wool, daub, feathers, thread) to 999, an estimate: the game limits what fits by weight and no public source gives a stack cap. Money, tools, food and clothing are left alone.
= 將背包中的基礎建築和製作材料（木材、石頭、黏土、皮革、稻草、鹽、毛皮、羊毛、抹灰泥、羽毛、線）提升到 999，此為估計值：遊戲按重量限制容量，且沒有公開來源說明堆疊上限。金錢、工具、食物和衣物不受影響。

Raises the six nano-element and polymer crafting materials you already have to 9,999. The game's stack figure for them is 1,000 per slot but real saves hold more, so the cap is an estimate. Gold, cores, bait, grenades, potions, gear and key items are left alone.
= 將已擁有的六種奈米元素和聚合物製作材料提升到 9,999。遊戲中每格的堆疊數為 1,000，但實際存檔中有更多，因此該上限為估計值。金幣、核心、魚餌、手雷、藥水、裝備和關鍵物品不受影響。

Rangers

Rank
= 等級

Rank 0 is Squaddie… 7 is Colonel. Stats are the soldier's own, before armour and items.
= 等級 0 為 Squaddie……7 為 Colonel。屬性為士兵自身數值，不含護甲和物品加成。

Rank goes from 0 (Intern) up; the game works it out again from experience after a round. Credits, the quota and the ship are in LCSaveFile1 to LCSaveFile3. Checked on public saves, not yet tried in the game.
= 等級從 0（Intern）起；每輪結束後遊戲會根據經驗重新計算。積分、配額和飛船位於 LCSaveFile1 至 LCSaveFile3。已在公開存檔上驗證，尚未在遊戲中試用。

Rank is the staff member's peak rank (S++ is the top). The six team stats follow from it, the stat distribution and bonus, medals and morale. The rest of each member's fields are in the Tree.
= 等級為員工的最高等級（S++ 最高）。六項部門屬性由等級、屬性分配與加成、勳章和士氣決定。每位成員的其餘欄位位於樹狀檢視中。

Rank S++, top stat bonus, all three medals, morale and proficiency at 15, the stat cap removed, and specialist stat types switched to the member's own team: in game this gives S++ in all six stats. Rank alone leaves some stats lower.
= 等級 S++、屬性加成最高、三枚勳章全部授予、士氣與熟練度為 15、解除屬性上限，並將專家型屬性分配改為所屬部門型別：遊戲中六項屬性均為 S++。只改等級會讓部分屬性偏低。

SAVEGAMEN.sav in the Steam remote folder. Each of the five playable characters keeps their own money, experience and skill orbs. The save has no checksum; close the game first.
= Steam remote 資料夾中的 SAVEGAMEN.sav。五名可玩角色各自擁有獨立的金錢、經驗和技能珠。該存檔沒有總和檢查碼;請先關閉遊戲。

Save count
= 存檔次數

Save slots
= 存檔位

SaveData-<n>_Character_<slot>.sav. Ergo held is what you can spend; the level is kept with the stats, so raise both together.
= SaveData-<n>_Character_<slot>.sav。「Ergo held」是可花費的數量;等級與屬性一併儲存,請同時提高兩者。

SaveData.bin: eight Mania Mode and three Encore Mode slots. Emeralds are a bit field (127 = all seven).
= SaveData.bin:八個 Mania Mode 存檔位和三個 Encore Mode 存檔位。翡翠為位欄位(127 = 全部七顆)。

SaveGame00.sav / SaveGameNN.sav. The zlib-packed actor records are opened and packed again on save.
= SaveGame00.sav / SaveGameNN.sav。經 zlib 壓縮的角色記錄會被解開,並在儲存時重新壓縮。

SaveGameData0.sav (the SAVE header and zlib packing are handled). pg, difficulty, each party member's level, experience, HP, MP and special ability points, and the item counts.
= SaveGameData0.sav(已處理 SAVE 頭和 zlib 壓縮)。pg、難度,以及每位隊員的等級、經驗、HP、MP 和特殊能力點數,還有物品數量。

SaveN.rxdata. Items, weapons and armour are listed by their number in the game's database (Data/Items.rxdata), with how many you carry. HP and SP above the maximum are cut back by the game. The save keeps the party and every actor as separate copies, so change a party member in both tables.
= SaveN.rxdata。物品、武器和護甲按其在遊戲資料庫(Data/Items.rxdata)中的編號列出,並顯示持有數量。超過上限的 HP 和 SP 會被遊戲削減。存檔將隊伍和每個角色分別儲存為獨立副本,因此請在兩個表中同時修改隊員。

Save_N_v10.as in the game's save folder (the game's own packing is opened and closed for you; the character file player.dat is shown). The party and the money are here; other entries are the levels. Rubles are the Money item in the player's inventory.
= 遊戲存檔資料夾中的 Save_N_v10.as(遊戲自帶的壓縮會自動解開並重新壓縮;顯示角色檔案 player.dat)。隊伍和金錢在這裡;其他條目為等級。盧布是玩家物品欄中的 Money 物品。

Set credits everywhere
= 全部設定信用點

Set each ammo type to what its SDU level holds (without relic or class mod bonuses).
= 將每種彈藥設為其 SDU 等級對應的數量(不含遺物或職業模組加成)。

Set every stack to 99
= 將每個堆疊設為 99

Set your treasury to 9,999,999
= 將國庫設為 9,999,999

Sets Raw Whalebone to 999. The game shows no limit and no public source states one, so 999 is a safe three-digit amount.
= 將 Raw Whalebone 設為 999。遊戲未顯示上限,也沒有公開資料給出上限,因此 999 是安全的三位數。

Sets all eight sepith to 9,999, the most the game holds.
= 將八種 Sepith 全部設為 9,999,即遊戲可容納的最大值。

Sets crafting parts to 999, the stack size players report (no official figure, so this is an estimate).
= 將製作零件設為 999,即玩家報告的堆疊上限(無官方資料,僅為估計)。

Sets each herb, feather and animal fat stack the satchel holds to 99, the most an item stacks to once the Legend of the East satchel is crafted. Rockstar publishes no smaller per-item limits; with smaller satchels the game may cut stacks down when the save loads.
= 將鞍囊中的每種草藥、羽毛和動物脂肪堆疊設為 99,這是製作出 Legend of the East 鞍囊後物品的最大堆疊數。Rockstar 未公佈各物品更小的上限;使用較小的鞍囊時,遊戲可能在讀取存檔時削減堆疊。

Sets each piece of scrap to a sell value of 200 if it is worth less.
= 將每件廢料的售價設為200（若低於此值）。

Sets every IV of the Pokémon in the party to 31; their stats are worked out again
= 將隊伍中寶可夢的全部個體值設為31；能力值會重新計算

Sets every area's fiends to 10 captured. The arena's own creations are left alone.
= 將每個區域的魔物捕獲數設為10。鬥技場的創造物不受影響。

Sets every synthesis material to 99, the stack the game's menus show as full.
= 將每種合成材料設為99，即遊戲選單中顯示的滿堆疊數量。

Sets leather, metal, chemicals and silk to 9,999. No public source states a limit, so this is an estimate. Unique materials such as black pearls are left alone.
= 將皮革、金屬、化學品和絲綢設為9,999。沒有公開資料給出上限，因此這是估計值。黑珍珠等獨特材料不受影響。

Sets lumber and material stone to 50,000, the most the game keeps. Fertilizer is left alone.
= 將木材和材料石設為50,000，即遊戲上限。肥料不受影響。

Sets money to 999,999, the most the game holds
= 將金錢設為999,999，即遊戲上限

Sets the six crafting materials to 99,999. No public source states a limit (real saves hold over 60,000), so this is an estimate.
= 將六種製作材料設為99,999。沒有公開資料給出上限（真實存檔中超過60,000），因此這是估計值。

Sets wood, metal and cloth to 2,000, what the Jackdaw's fully upgraded hold takes. Rum and sugar are trade goods and are left alone.
= 將木材、金屬和布料設為2,000，即完全升級後的Jackdaw貨艙容量。朗姆酒和糖是貿易品，不受影響。

Sets wood, metal, cloth and stone to 2,500, what the Morrigan's fully upgraded hold takes. Tobacco is a trade good and is left alone.
= 將木材、金屬、布料和石頭設為2,500，即完全升級後的Morrigan貨艙容量。菸草是貿易品，不受影響。

Stats
= 屬性

Stats are the base values before the dressphere and accessories.
= 屬性為應用 Dressphere 和飾品之前的基礎值。

= 屬性最高 255。遊戲會根據基礎上限和裝備重新計算最大 HP/MP,因此請提高基礎上限,以使更高的最大值得以保留。

Stress

= 遊戲不儲存為零的值，因此檔案中沒有可修改的內容。請先在遊戲中拾取一些護甲（或彈藥），然後再次存檔。

= 職業存檔槽中最新自動存檔資料夾內的career/playerAttributes.json（遊戲會載入輪換自動存檔中最新的一個，請編輯它）。每個分支（職業的技能線）各有自己的值，顯示在Tree標籤頁中。

datN.slv in the game's savedata folder (one file per character). Salt and gold are what the character carries. Items and the skill tree are not edited yet.
= 遊戲savedata資料夾中的datN.slv（每個角色一個檔案）。鹽和金幣為角色隨身攜帶的數量。物品和技能樹暫不可編輯。

defaultES2File.txt (items.txt holds the shop items). Money is in marks. The needs run from 0 (fine) to 100 (passing out); hunger, thirst and the rest are best set low.
= defaultES2File.txt（items.txt 儲存商店物品）。金錢單位為馬克。需求值範圍 0（良好）到 100（昏厥）；飢餓、口渴等建議設得低一些。

= ff13-NN.dat。編輯時會解密存檔，儲存時重新加密。Gil 上限為 9,999,999。

persistentgamedata1.dat (Rebirth), ab_ / abp_ (Afterbirth, Afterbirth+) or rep_ / rep+ (Repentance), in the Steam remote folder. Counters, achievements, collectibles seen and challenges; each unlock is 0 (locked) or 1 (unlocked), numbered as in the game's own lists. The checksum is written again on save.
= persistentgamedata1.dat（Rebirth）、ab_ / abp_（Afterbirth、Afterbirth+）或 rep_ / rep+（Repentance），位於 Steam 的 remote 資料夾。包含計數器、成就、已見收藏品和挑戰；每個解鎖項為 0（未解鎖）或 1（已解鎖），編號與遊戲內清單一致。儲存時會重新寫入總和檢查碼。

pg
= pg

pg (money)
= pg（金錢）

player.xml from the save00 folder (close the game first). Gold and health; the game shows health 25 times the stored value. Wands, spells and perks are entities inside the file, in the Tree.
= save00 資料夾中的 player.xml（請先關閉遊戲）。金幣和生命值；遊戲顯示的生命值為儲存值的 25 倍。法杖、法術和天賦是檔案內的實體，位於樹狀檢視中。

progress.save from the game's profile folder (plain JSON). Each character's highest Ascension, wins, losses, win streaks and play time, plus the profile's score. Discovered cards, relics and the rest are lists in the Tree.
= 遊戲配置資料夾中的 progress.save（純 JSON）。每個角色的最高進階、勝場、敗場、連勝和遊戲時間，以及配置檔案的分數。已發現的卡牌、遺物等是樹狀檢視中的清單。

quality
= 品質

rget Entity Id
= rget Entity Id

rget Size
= rget Size

saveData.ini (the file is a GameMaker ini; values are written as text like 1.000000). Scores are points, ranks are the letters d, c, b, a, s and p, treasure is 1 when found and secrets found are 0 to 3.
= saveData.ini（該檔案是 GameMaker 的 ini；值以 1.000000 這樣的文字寫入）。分數為點數，評級為字母 d、c、b、a、s 和 p，寶物找到時為 1，已找到的秘密為 0 至 3。

saveFile<n>.sav: level, ability points, life and energy cells, keystones, mapstones and every skill and ability-tree upgrade.
= saveFile<n>.sav：等級、能力點、生命和能量電池、關鍵石、地圖石，以及所有技能和能力樹升級。

save_auto.bin and the other .bin saves in the game's save folder (each is a zip; the text inside is opened for you). Weapons and upgrades are in the Tree tab under PlayerTemplate.
= 遊戲存檔資料夾中的 save_auto.bin 及其他 .bin 存檔（均為 zip，其中的文字會自動開啟）。武器和升級位於「樹」選項卡的 PlayerTemplate 下。

savedata.bin of the Steam edition. Money buys and upgrades weapons; exchange points buy the Bonus Features (costumes, filters, infinite ammo, figures).
= Steam 版的 savedata.bin。金錢用於購買和升級武器；兌換點數用於購買獎勵功能（服裝、濾鏡、無限彈藥、手辦）。

savegame.dsa in a savegame_N folder (a SQLite database; the screenshot and .nfo beside it are not needed). Heroes with a party slot of 0 or more are in your party; the other rows are the characters in cutscenes and other places. Adventure points to spend are in "Spendable AP". Items and money are in the Tree.
= savegame_N 資料夾中的 savegame.dsa（SQLite 資料庫；旁邊的截圖和 .nfo 不需要）。隊伍位為 0 或更大的英雄在您的隊伍中；其餘行是過場動畫及其他地點的角色。可花費的冒險點數在「Spendable AP」中。物品和金錢位於樹狀檢視中。

savegameN.sav in the Steam remote folder. Each of the four playable characters keeps their own money, experience and skill orbs. The save has no checksum; close the game first.
= Steam remote 資料夾中的 savegameN.sav。四名可玩角色各自擁有獨立的金錢、經驗和技能寶珠。存檔沒有總和檢查碼；請先關閉遊戲。

savegameN.sav in the Steam remote folder. The save has no checksum; close the game first.
= Steam remote 資料夾中的 savegameN.sav。存檔沒有總和檢查碼；請先關閉遊戲。

savegame_N.save (base64-wrapped JSON, opened and closed for you). Tears of Atonement, current health and fervour, the upgrade levels of each stat, flask count and guilt. Owned rosary beads, prayers, relics and sword hearts are lists of ids in the Tree.
= savegame_N.save（base64 封裝的 JSON，會自動開啟和關閉）。贖罪之淚、目前生命值和狂熱值、各屬性的升級等級、藥瓶數量和罪孽。已擁有的念珠、祈禱、遺物和劍之心是樹狀檢視中的 ID 清單。

<n>.sav in %LOCALAPPDATA%\\AtomicHeart\\Saved\\SaveGames. The save's compressed data is unpacked and packed again with the compression library of any game on this PC, and its SHA-1 is made again. Only stacks the save already holds are listed: pick up one of an item in the game first to add it.
= %LOCALAPPDATA%\AtomicHeart\Saved\SaveGames 中的 <n>.sav。存檔的壓縮資料會用本機任一遊戲的壓縮庫解包並重新打包，並重新生成其SHA-1。僅列出存檔中已有的堆疊：請先在遊戲中拾取一個該物品以新增。

PS4 and Switch saves of the same release use the same format.

SRDR3<nnnn> in Documents\\Rockstar Games\\Red Dead Redemption 2\\Profiles\\<id> (story mode, PC). The save is decrypted, and its checksums are written again and it is encrypted when you save. Satchel stacks can be changed, and supplies the satchel lacks can be added (Add to satchel: an experimental step that grows the save; keep a backup). Weapons keep their own loaded ammo, which is left alone. Challenge ranks and compendium ticks change the record only: they do not hand out rewards. Compendium entries whose name is not known yet are shown by their number. Honor and camp funds also update the matching saved statistic. Turn Steam/Rockstar cloud saves off for the game first, or the old save may come back.
= Documents\Rockstar Games\Red Dead Redemption 2\Profiles\<id> 中的 SRDR3<nnnn>(故事模式,PC)。存檔會被解密,總和檢查碼會重新寫入,儲存時再加密。可修改鞍囊堆疊,也可新增鞍囊中缺少的補給(新增到鞍囊:會增大存檔的實驗性步驟,請先備份)。武器保留各自已裝填的彈藥,不會被改動。挑戰等級和圖鑑勾選只改變記錄:不會發放獎勵。名稱尚未知的圖鑑條目以編號顯示。榮譽和營地資金也會更新對應的已儲存統計。請先關閉該遊戲的 Steam/Rockstar 雲存檔,否則舊存檔可能會回來。

ArchiveSaveFile.<n>.sav from b1\Saved\SaveGames\<Steam id>. Will, Sparks, level, experience and play time; the file's checksum is written again when you save. Close the game first and keep the backup. Raising the level does not add Sparks or experience for you.

# src/fmt (first-batch screens)
Close the game and turn off Steam cloud saves first. The game's two checksums are rewritten. Choosing a difficulty writes every place the game keeps it.
= 請先關閉遊戲並關閉 Steam 雲存檔。遊戲的兩個總和檢查碼會被重新寫入。選擇難度時會寫入遊戲儲存難度的每一處。

Close the game first. Counts of items already in the bag are edited; setting a count to 0 leaves the slot in the bag. The save has no checksum.
= 請先關閉遊戲。可編輯背包中已有物品的數量；將數量設為 0 會在背包中保留該格。該存檔沒有總和檢查碼。

= 請先關閉遊戲。兩個加密塊會用各自的種子重新寫入。武器元素和武將按編號顯示；只列出已有的元素（1 個及以上）和有進度的武將。

= 請先關閉遊戲。流密碼、四個分割槽總和檢查碼以及字總和檢查碼會被重新寫入。武將按其在存檔中的編號顯示；等級和經驗為只讀。

No crafting materials in {0}.
= {0}中沒有製作材料。

Painting changes blocks, walls and liquids; furniture, chests, trees and other objects are left alone. Each stroke is one undo step. Liquid only fills empty tiles and settles when the game runs.
= 繪製會更改方塊、牆壁和液體；傢俱、箱子、樹木等物體保持不變。每一筆為一個撤銷步驟。液體只會填充空格子，並在遊戲執行時自行流動穩定。

Painting changes what fills each cell: its element, mass, temperature and germs. Buildings, critters, plants and debris are left alone, and cells with a tile built in them aren't painted. Neutronium is kept unless you tick “Also change Neutronium”. Each stroke is one undo step. Liquids and gases settle when the game runs.
= 繪製會更改每個格子的內容：元素、質量、溫度和細菌。建築、生物、植物和雜物保持不變，已建有磚塊的格子不會被繪製。除非勾選「同時更改中子素」，否則中子素會保留。每一筆為一個撤銷步驟。液體和氣體會在遊戲執行時自行穩定。

The game stores each unlock as that entry's own code (a plain “yes” unlocks nothing once a run ends). {0} codes are known; entries without a known code stay as they are. Each entry can also be edited in the Values tab.
= 遊戲將每個解鎖項儲存為該條目自己的程式碼（單純的「yes」在一局結束後不會解鎖任何內容）。已知 {0} 個程式碼；沒有已知程式碼的條目保持原樣。每個條目也可在「值」標籤頁中編輯。

The game's own record of how you played: kills per area decide the Genocide and Neutral endings, and the flags below them mark the fights you won. Flags that disagree with each other (a Pacifist ending with kills counted) give odd dialogue. Most flags are 0 or 1.
= 遊戲自身對你遊玩方式的記錄：各區域的擊殺數決定滅絕線和中立線結局，其下的標記表示你贏下的戰鬥。互相矛盾的標記（例如已有擊殺數卻是和平結局）會導致奇怪的對話。大多數標記為 0 或 1。

These are the flags the game checks (progress, NPCs moving in, the Bestiary and achievements aren't changed).
= 這些是遊戲檢查的標記（進度、NPC 入住、怪物圖鑑和成就不會被更改）。

Count is the uses left (chalk, climbing tape) or the sips left in a bottle; Contents is what a bottle, flask or thermos holds. Pick an item in an empty square to add it: it goes at the bottom of the bag, and “Shake up bag” in the game settles everything. Set an item to (none) to take it out.
= Count 是剩餘使用次數（鎂粉、攀巖膠帶）或瓶中剩餘的口數；Contents 是瓶子、水壺或保溫杯裡裝的東西。在空格中選擇物品即可新增：它會放在背包最下方，遊戲設定中的「Shake up bag」會把一切整理好。將物品設為 (none) 即可移除。

Every Smithing Stone and Somber Smithing Stone (Ancient Dragon ones too) for weapons, and every Grave and Ghost Glovewort for Spirit Ashes, each to the 999 you can hold.
= 武器用的所有 Smithing Stone 和 Somber Smithing Stone（含 Ancient Dragon），以及靈灰用的所有 Grave Glovewort 和 Ghost Glovewort，各補到可攜帶的 999 個。

Every kind of titanite, Dragon Scales and Demon Titanite, each to the 99 you can carry.
= 所有種類的 Titanite、Dragon Scale 和 Demon Titanite，各補到可攜帶的 99 個。

Gear is read from each item's serial (level and its check); names need the game's part tables, so they aren't shown, and gear isn't edited. {0} item(s) in the bank.
= 裝備從每件物品的序列號中讀取（等級及其校驗）；名稱需要遊戲的零件表，因此不顯示，裝備也無法編輯。銀行中有 {0} 件物品。

How many of each item this character carries directly (items inside bags aren't listed), named by the game's item entry. Change a stack size, or add items below; the game may trim a stack to what the item allows.
= 該角色直接攜帶的每種物品數量（包內物品不列出），以遊戲的物品條目命名。可更改堆疊數量，或在下方新增物品；遊戲可能會把堆疊削減到該物品允許的數量。

= 選擇 (empty) 可移除你在此新增的物品。已裝備或在快捷欄中的物品無法更換，武器等級也會保持在其允許的範圍內。

Pick who holds each Djinni, set or on standby, or nobody. The game works out class and Psynergy from the set Djinn the next time you change Djinn in its own menu, so open the Djinn menu and set one again after loading. Djinn the story hands out are not marked as collected in the world: the game may offer them again.
= 選擇每隻 Djinni 由誰持有：裝備中、待命或無人持有。下次在 Djinn 選單中更換 Djinn 時，遊戲會根據裝備中的 Djinn 計算職業和 Psynergy，所以讀取存檔後請開啟 Djinn 選單並重新設定一隻。劇情贈送的 Djinn 不會在世界中標記為已收集：遊戲可能會再次提供。

Scrap Iron, Black and Yellow Gunpowder, Scrap Magnetite, Adamantite Scrap, Fulminated Mercury, Fat and Grave Wax and Lapis Lazuli, each to the 99 you can hold.
= Scrap Iron、Black 和 Yellow Gunpowder、Scrap Magnetite、Adamantite Scrap、Fulminated Mercury、Fat 和 Grave Wax 以及 Lapis Lazuli，各補到可攜帶的 99 個。

Seconds left is the time the game shows for the buff (10:00 is 600). Set it to 0 to end a buff, or give an empty one time to start it with its usual source and strength. Sips taken one after another stack and run in turn; a changed buff is saved as one with the whole time. Stamina and Strength show on the game's Grip line (it shows the longest of them and Grip). Extra grip comes from chalk and doesn't run down off the wall (the chalk count on the HUD is the chalk itself: 12 grips a charge). Strength and Stoked! can only be changed when the save already has them.
= Seconds left 是遊戲為增益顯示的剩餘時間（10:00 即 600）。設為 0 可結束增益，給空的增益填入時間則會以其常見來源和強度啟動。連續喝下的幾口會疊加並依次生效；修改後的增益會儲存為總時長的單個增益。Stamina 和 Strength 顯示在遊戲的 Grip 行中（它顯示其中最長的一項和 Grip）。額外的抓力來自鎂粉，離開巖壁也不會消耗（HUD 上的鎂粉數就是鎂粉本身：每份 12 次抓握）。Strength 和 Stoked! 僅在存檔已有時才能修改。

Skill points to spend are the tile above. Skill experience fills toward the next point; the game hands it out once it's full.
= 可用的技能點在上方的卡片中。技能經驗會累積到下一點，滿了之後由遊戲發放。

Stack sizes of what this character carries, by the game's item name. Items can't be removed here; Add items adds new ones. A stack the game split just before saving shows as read-only.
= 此角色所攜帶物品的堆疊數量，按遊戲的物品名稱顯示。此處無法移除物品；可在「新增道具」中新增新物品。遊戲在儲存前剛拆分的堆疊會顯示為只讀。

Clears dead, unconscious, coma and incapacitated and ends bleeding. Raise any part below zero above it too, or the character falls again.
= 清除死亡、昏迷、昏睡和失能狀態並止血。同時把低於零的部位提升到零以上，否則角色會再次倒下。

Stats are as the Sphere Grid has raised them (255 is the most). HP above 9999 needs Break HP Limit. The game works the totals out again from the "without equipment" numbers, so change both. Sphere levels are the moves left to spend on the Sphere Grid.
= 屬性值為晶球盤提升後的數值（最大 255）。HP 超過 9999 需要 Break HP Limit。遊戲會根據「不含裝備」的數值重新計算總值，因此兩者都要修改。晶球等級是晶球盤上剩餘可用的移動次數。

Changing story flags and choices can break quests, soft-lock the game or stop it from being completed. Treat it as experimental, with no guarantee the save can still be finished, and use it only if you know what you're doing. Keep a backup first so you can go back (Backups › Open save manager restores it). The file as it was is also backed up automatically when you save.
= 更改劇情標記和選擇可能會破壞任務、使遊戲卡死或無法通關。請視為實驗性功能，不保證存檔仍可完成，僅在您清楚自己在做什麼時使用。請先備份以便還原（備份 › 開啟存檔管理器 可恢復）。儲存時也會自動備份原檔案。

Level and difficulty here are what the load screen shows. Gold, experience, talent points and the inventory are kept in the game's compressed database inside this save, which can't be edited yet.
= 這裡的等級和難度是讀檔介面顯示的值。金幣、經驗、天賦點和物品欄儲存在此存檔內遊戲的壓縮資料庫中，目前還無法編輯。

Click a flag to turn it on or off. These are the game's own story records: changing one doesn't replay the scene behind it, and flags that contradict each other (alive and dead, two romances) can confuse later scenes, so change few at a time and keep the backup.
= 點選標記即可開啟或關閉。這些是遊戲自己的劇情記錄：修改後不會重播對應的場景，互相矛盾的標記（既活著又死亡、兩段戀情）可能讓之後的場景出錯，因此每次只改少量，並保留備份。

Rank 0 is Rookie … 7 is Colonel (8 Brigadier with some classes). A stat sets the soldier's current, maximum and base value together, so a wounded soldier is healed to it. A blank cell is a value the game left at its default.
= 軍銜 0 為新兵……7 為上校（部分職業 8 為準將）。設定一項屬性會同時設定士兵的目前值、最大值和基礎值，因此受傷計程車兵會被治癒到該值。空白單元格表示遊戲保留預設值。

Characters are numbered in the order the save keeps them (Cloud is usually first). The game can recalculate max HP and MP from level and equipment, so raise the level and experience together for lasting changes.
= 角色按存檔儲存的順序編號（通常克勞德排第一）。遊戲可能根據等級和裝備重新計算最大 HP 和 MP，因此請同時提升等級和經驗，才能讓修改保持。

Each materia is listed with its slot number, since a save often has several of one kind. Materia someone has equipped can't be removed. In Rebirth, level and AP can be changed up to the materia's top level.
= 每顆魔晶石都會列出其槽位編號，因為存檔裡常有多顆同類魔晶石。已被裝備的魔晶石無法移除。在《重生》中，等級和 AP 最高可改到該魔晶石的最高等級。

Items and materials go up to 99. Removing frees the slot when you save. Key items, currencies, outfits, folios and trophies are shown but can't be removed. Armor and accessories someone is wearing can't be removed (in Rebirth they can't be removed at all, only changed in number).
= 物品和材料最多 99 個。移除後，儲存時會釋放該欄位。關鍵物品、貨幣、服裝、圖鑑冊和獎盃會顯示，但無法移除。有人正在穿戴的護甲和飾品無法移除（在《重生》中完全無法移除，只能修改數量）。

Hand a civilisation to a human or the AI, or change whose turn it is — for hotseat or play-by-cloud games when someone drops out. Gold, faith, cities and units are in the packed part of the save, which isn't mapped yet.
= 可以把某個文明交給人類玩家或 AI，或更改輪到誰行動——適用於熱座或雲端對戰中有人退出的情況。黃金、信仰、城市和單位位於存檔的壓縮部分，目前尚未解析。

Pieces this save doesn't have at any level. They're added at their base level when you save; upgrade them at a shop in the game. Up to 32 at a time. Keep a copy of game.sav until you've loaded the save in the game.
= 此存檔中任何等級都沒有的裝備。儲存時會以基礎等級新增，請在遊戲內商店中升級。一次最多 32 件。在遊戲中成功讀取存檔之前，請保留一份 game.sav 的副本。

Pieces you don't have yet, at the level you pick, for each type you already own a piece of (that piece's record is the model; it stays as it is). Added when you save, up to 32 at a time. Keep a copy of the save until you've loaded it in the game.
= 追加你尚未擁有的裝備，等級由你選擇，針對你已擁有至少一件的每種型別（該裝備的記錄會作為模板，保持不變）。儲存時新增，一次最多 32 件。在遊戲中成功讀取存檔之前，請保留存檔副本。

An emulator's or Dolphin's .gci save. Per Mario file: lives, which nozzles and Yoshi are unlocked, and for every area the shine sprites, blue coins, events and scores. The shine sprite total is kept in step.
= 模擬器或 Dolphin 的 .gci 存檔。每個馬里奧存檔:生命數、已解鎖的噴水裝置和耀西,以及每個區域的閃耀星星、藍色金幣、事件和分數。閃耀星星總數會同步更新。

An emulator's or Dolphin's .gci save. Per slot: health, magic, rupees, the equipment and wallet, every item and bag, the quantities, the figurines, the dungeons' maps, compasses and keys, and the events (Big Octos, mini-games, postbox and more). The sword, shield and bracelet flags follow the equipment you pick.
= 模擬器或 Dolphin 的 .gci 存檔。每個存檔位:體力、魔力、盧比、裝備和錢包、每件道具和袋子、數量、人偶、各迷宮的地圖、指南針和鑰匙,以及事件(大章魚、小遊戲、郵筒等)。劍、盾和手鐲的標記會跟隨你選擇的裝備。

Close the emulator first. Health is in quarter hearts, so 12 is three hearts. Changing the sword, shield or bracelets also sets the matching equipped flag. Each edited slot's sum is made again.
= 請先關閉模擬器。體力以四分之一顆心為單位,所以 12 就是三顆心。更改劍、盾或手鐲時,對應的裝備標記也會同步設定。每個已編輯存檔位的總和檢查碼會重新計算。

= 請先關閉模擬器。房間和 Boo 都是標記:勾選為已訪問的房間不會自動出現在地圖上。每個已編輯區段的總和檢查碼會重新計算。

= 請先關閉遊戲。加法總和檢查碼會重新寫入。武將按存檔中的編號顯示;升級可能會重置其能力值。

= 請先關閉遊戲。加法總和檢查碼會重新寫入。只列出已解鎖的武將;能力值上限是估算值,並非遊戲本身的上限。

= 請先關閉遊戲。存檔會用你的Steam帳號編號解密,並在儲存時重新加密。只能開啟在「我的城」中建立的存檔(非戰鬥中)。

{0} kinds of item to add
= 要新增的物品種類：{0}

Every kind of item found in an inventory in this save can be added, as a copy of one the game made. Items go into the chosen character's bag when you save; where they already carry a stack of the same item, that stack grows instead. Keys, containers and story items aren't offered. Keep a backup: this hasn't been tried in the game yet.
= 本存檔中任何物品欄裡出現過的物品種類都可以新增，作為遊戲所生成物品的副本。儲存時物品會放入所選角色的背包；如果該角色已攜帶同一物品的堆疊，則改為增加該堆疊。不提供鑰匙、容器和劇情物品。請保留備份：此功能尚未在遊戲中試過。

Close the emulator first. Each Z-item and each unlocked character also keeps an obtained flag, written again on save, and the save's checksum is written again.
= 請先關閉模擬器。每個Z物品和每個已解鎖的角色還帶有「已獲得」標記，儲存時會重新寫入，存檔的總和檢查碼也會重新寫入。

Close the emulator first. The game keeps no checksum. A skill is learned by each character whose box says Yes; items in a character's pack can be anything the game knows (equipment, key items and consumables share one list).
= 請先關閉模擬器。遊戲沒有總和檢查碼。每個標記為「是」的角色都學會了對應的技能；角色背包中的物品可以是遊戲已知的任何物品（裝備、關鍵物品和消耗品共用一個清單）。

Close the emulator first. The save file holds 16 slots; only the used ones are listed. The game ranks each fighter from the Bouncer Points spent on stats and skills, so the rank and the points spent are written again when you change a stat or a skill; each changed slot's checksum is written again too.
= 請先關閉模擬器。存檔檔案包含16個存檔位，只列出已使用的。遊戲根據花在屬性和技能上的保鏢點數給每位角色評級，因此修改屬性或技能時，評級和已花費點數會重新寫入；每個被修改存檔位的總和檢查碼也會重新寫入。

Close the emulator first. This reads the USA and European saves; the game keeps no checksum. A demon's own identity, its resistances and the magatama are not edited here; item stocks are capped at 99.
= 請先關閉模擬器。支援美版和歐版存檔，遊戲沒有總和檢查碼。惡魔本身的種類、抗性和勾玉在此不可編輯；道具庫存上限為99。

Search again
= 重新搜尋

The saves found before are remembered and checked first. Search again looks through all the usual places on this computer for games added since.
= 之前找到的存檔會被記住並優先檢查。「重新搜尋」會遍歷此電腦上所有常見位置，查詢之後新增的遊戲。

Every kind of item in your party's bags can be added, as a copy of one the game made. The copies go into the chosen character's bag when you save. Items kept in a stack, worn gear, containers, keys and story items aren't offered; a stack's size can be changed under Stacked items. Keep a backup: this hasn't been tried in the game yet.
= 隊伍背包中的任何種類物品都可以新增，作為遊戲所建立物品的副本。儲存時副本會放入所選角色的背包。堆疊物品、已穿戴的裝備、容器、鑰匙和劇情物品不在可選範圍內；堆疊數量可在「堆疊物品」中修改。請先備份：此功能尚未在遊戲中測試。

Lifetime reward points are what the Rewards store spends; lifetime happiness is the total the Sim has ever earned. Needs go from -100 to 100 and are only kept for Sims the game has loaded. Job and school performance go from -100 to 100; celebrity level from 0 to 5. Every skill goes up to its own highest level (toddler and child skills to 1 or 3); a few expansion skills whose ids couldn't be matched to a name aren't listed. The supernatural type is shown but can't be changed here: turning a Sim into one needs data the game creates itself. Funds go up to 99,999,999. The game data is stored uncompressed when you save, so the file grows. Not yet tried in the game.
= 終身獎勵點數是獎勵商店消耗的點數；終身幸福值是模擬市民累計獲得的總量。需求範圍為 -100 到 100，只為遊戲已載入的模擬市民儲存。工作和學業表現範圍為 -100 到 100；名人等級為 0 到 5。每項技能可升到各自的最高等級（幼兒和兒童技能為 1 或 3）；少數無法對應到名稱的資料片技能未列出。超自然型別僅顯示，無法在此更改：變身需要遊戲自己建立的資料。資金上限為 99,999,999。儲存時遊戲資料以未壓縮形式儲存，因此檔案會變大。尚未在遊戲中測試。

# src/game_ui/simple/hardpc_specs.rs (Bug Fables, Planetbase)
save0.dat, save1.dat or save2.dat in the game's folder (the PC release). Berries, rank, experience, TP, MP, the inventory and storage sizes and play time, and the HP, attack and defense of every party member. Only these values are rewritten; the rest of the save is kept as it was.
= 遊戲資料夾中的 save0.dat、save1.dat 或 save2.dat（PC 版）。漿果、等級、經驗、TP、MP、背包和倉庫格數、遊戲時間，以及每名隊員的 HP、攻擊和防禦。只重寫這些數值，存檔的其餘部分保持原樣。

A .sav file in Documents\\Planetbase\\Saves (plain XML). Coins (once the colony has traded), extra prestige, the landing permissions, and the health, food, water, oxygen, sleep and morale of every colonist and the condition of every bot, from 0 to 1. Goods in storage are single items on the ground and are not listed.
= Documents\Planetbase\Saves 中的 .sav 檔案（純 XML）。金幣（殖民地開始交易後才有）、額外聲望、著陸許可，以及每名殖民者的健康、營養、水分、氧氣、睡眠和士氣，和每個機器人的狀態，範圍 0 到 1。倉儲物資是地面上的單個物品，不在清單中。

# src/game_ui/simple/n64_specs.rs

All-Night Mask
= 不眠面具

An emulator's .fla or .srm save, or the GameCube .gci. Per slot: rupees, health, magic, equipment and ammunition, every item, mask, song and boss remains, each temple's stray fairies, keys and dungeon items, and the owl statues.
= 模擬器的 .fla 或 .srm 存檔，或 GameCube 的 .gci。每個存檔位：盧比、生命、魔力、裝備與彈藥、所有物品、面具、歌曲和頭目遺骸、各神殿的迷途妖精、鑰匙和迷宮道具，以及貓頭鷹石像。

An emulator's .fla or .srm save. Per file: coins, Star Pieces, Star Points, level, HP, FP and BP with their maximums, Star Spirits, boots and hammer, the item bag, the partners and their ranks, and the battle records. Badges, stored and key items, recipes and star pieces found are in the Tree.
= 模擬器的 .fla 或 .srm 存檔。每個檔案：金幣、星之碎片、星星點數、等級、HP、FP 和 BP 及其上限、星之精靈、靴子和錘子、道具欄、同伴及其等級，以及戰鬥記錄。徽章、寄存物品和重要物品、食譜以及找到的星之碎片在樹狀檢視中。

Close the emulator first. Health is in sixteenths of a heart. When a slot has an owl-statue save, that is the save the game loads, so it is the one shown and edited. Each edited save's sum is made again and its backup copy is updated.

Close the emulator first. The newest save of each file is the one shown. Max HP and Max FP include the HP Plus and FP Plus badges worn; the base values are worked out from them on save. Both sums of each edited save are made again.
= 請先關閉模擬器。顯示的是每個檔案最新的存檔。最大 HP 和最大 FP 包含已裝備的 HP Plus 與 FP Plus 徽章；儲存時據此算出基礎值。每個編輯過的存檔的兩個總和檢查碼都會重新計算。

Risky

Romance state: later scenes check it, and contradicting flags can confuse them.
= 戀愛狀態：後續場景會檢查它，相互矛盾的標記可能使場景出錯。

Someone lives or dies by this flag. The character itself isn't brought back or killed, so scenes can contradict what you see.
= 此標記決定某人的生死。角色本身不會因此復活或死亡，因此場景可能與你看到的情況相矛盾。

The game mode (difficulty and the like) is also kept elsewhere in the save; this flag alone doesn't change it.
= 遊戲模式（難度等）也儲存在存檔的其他位置；僅改此標記不會改變它。

The tadpole and mind flayer story; key scenes depend on it.
= 蝌蚪與奪心魔的劇情；關鍵場景依賴於它。

Tick a flag to set it, untick it to clear it. These are the story's global flags, named as the game's scripts name them; the story script's own list of set flags is kept in step when you save. Changing a flag doesn't replay the scene behind it. Flags marked ⚠ are riskier than most, so change few at a time and keep the backup.
= 勾選即設定標記，取消勾選即清除。這些是劇情的全域性標記，名稱與遊戲腳本中一致；儲存時會同步更新劇情腳本自己的已設定標記清單。更改標記不會重播其背後的場景。帶 ⚠ 的標記風險較高，請一次只改少量，並保留備份。

Who is in the party or at camp is also kept elsewhere in the save; changing only the flag can leave a companion half in, half out.
= 誰在隊伍或營地中也儲存在存檔的其他位置；只改標記可能讓同伴處於半加入半離開的狀態。

Documents\\Ghost of Tsushima DIRECTOR'S CUT\\<id>\\manual_NNNN.sav or auto.sav: supplies, flowers, Ghost Flowers, crafting materials and technique points to spend. The save's checksum is made again on save.
= Documents\\Ghost of Tsushima DIRECTOR'S CUT\\<id>\\manual_NNNN.sav 或 auto.sav：物資、花、戰鬼之花、製作材料和可用技能點。儲存時會重新計算存檔的總和檢查碼。

Raises supplies to 9999 and bamboo, yew wood, wax wood, iron, steel, gold, linen, leather, silk and predator hides to 500 (fan wikis give 500 as the carry limit and full saves hold 500; the game states no limit itself). Amounts above that are kept. Flowers and Ghost Flowers are left alone.
= 將物資提高到 9999,將竹子、紫杉木、漆樹木、鐵、鋼、金、亞麻、皮革、絲綢和猛獸皮提高到 500(粉絲維基稱攜帶上限為 500,完成度高的存檔也是 500;遊戲本身未說明上限)。已超過的數量保持不變。花和戰鬼之花不做改動。

Supplies, flowers, crafting materials and technique points. The checksum is made again on save. Checked on public saves, not yet tried in the game.

= 以 JSON 開啟：解鎖項、進度和屬性，每個數值按名稱列出。存檔的 AES 加密會被去除，並在儲存時重新套上（適用於 1.0.17 及以前版本的存檔；尚未在真實存檔上測試）

Opens as an Unreal save: every property by name, numbers and text changed in place (no save of this game has been tried yet)
= 以 Unreal 存檔開啟：每個屬性按名稱列出，數字和文字就地修改（尚未測試過該遊戲的任何存檔）

Opens as an Unreal save: the packed save data is unpacked, every property shown by name in sections, numbers and text changed in place, and the save's checksum written again (no save of this game has been tried yet)
= 以 Unreal 存檔開啟：打包的存檔資料會被解包，每個屬性按名稱分節顯示，數字和文字就地修改，存檔的總和檢查碼會重新寫入（尚未測試過該遊戲的任何存檔）

Opens in the Hex view for Value search (find your stud count, then change it); the older TT Games saves are not encrypted (checked on LEGO Marvel Super Heroes). No dedicated screen yet

The save's SHA-1 checksum and zlib packing are handled, so the unpacked data can be changed in the hex view or found with the value search (cells, gold) and saved back for the game to accept. Checked on test files, not yet on a real save
= 存檔的 SHA-1 總和檢查碼與 zlib 壓縮都會被處理，因此解包後的資料可在十六進位檢視中修改，或透過數值搜尋查詢（cells、金幣），並儲存回去讓遊戲接受。已在測試檔案上檢查，尚未在真實存檔上測試

Unpacked for Value search and packed again on save (the header, chunk layout and footer are kept). Players' gold and influence are on the Tree for the game versions that keep them in the known place. Checked on public sample saves for unpack and repack; not yet tried in the game
= 會解包以供數值搜尋，並在儲存時重新打包（頭部、資料塊佈局和尾部都會保留）。對於將玩家金幣和影響力儲存在已知位置的遊戲版本，它們會顯示在樹狀檢視中。已在公開的樣本存檔上檢查解包和重新打包；尚未在遊戲中測試

= 裝備全部技能

Equips every skill in this save, including the ones you haven't learned. The game normally limits what you equip by your SP; it doesn't check that when it loads the save.
= 裝備此存檔中的所有技能，包括尚未習得的技能。遊戲通常按 SP 限制可裝備的技能，但讀取存檔時不會檢查。

Every skill is already equipped.
= 所有技能都已裝備。

# The Witcher 2, DOOM Eternal
Decrypted with your Steam account and encrypted again on save; profile.bin opens for Value search and game.details as text (its checksum isn't worked out, so leave it alone). The level saves (game_duration.dat) are packed again inside and aren't opened further
= 用你的 Steam 帳戶解密，儲存時重新加密；profile.bin 可用於數值搜尋，game.details 以文字開啟（其總和檢查碼不會重新計算，請不要修改）。關卡存檔（game_duration.dat）內部還經過一次壓縮，不會進一步開啟

Game screen: Geralt's experience, unspent talent points and Orens. The save's DZIP container is unpacked and packed again. Checked on several saves, not yet tried in the game
= 遊戲畫面：傑洛特的經驗、未使用的天賦點和奧倫。存檔的 DZIP 容器會被解包並重新打包。已在多個存檔上檢查，尚未在遊戲中試過

ManualSave_N.sav, AutoSave_N.sav or QuickSave.sav from Documents\\Witcher 2\\gamesaves. Geralt's experience, unspent talent points and Orens. The save is unpacked and packed again when you save. Close the game first.
= Documents\\Witcher 2\\gamesaves 中的 ManualSave_N.sav、AutoSave_N.sav 或 QuickSave.sav。傑洛特的經驗、未使用的天賦點和奧倫。儲存時會解包並重新打包存檔。請先關閉遊戲。

Orens
= 奧倫

# data/games, src/games.rs: Pillars of Eternity

<id> <name>.savegame (the Microsoft Store version keeps MobileObjects.save in a folder)
= <ID> <名稱>.savegame（Microsoft Store 版本將 MobileObjects.save 放在一個資料夾中）

A .savegame from Saved Games\\Pillars of Eternity (a zip; its MobileObjects.save is edited and the rest is kept), or the MobileObjects.save of the Microsoft Store version. Money, camping supplies, the difficulty, each party member's experience, attributes and skills, and the size of every stack you carry or keep in the stash. Skills count the skill points spent on them. Levels go up in the game once the experience is there. Everything else is in the Tree. Close the game first.
= 來自 Saved Games\\Pillars of Eternity 的 .savegame（一個 zip；編輯其中的 MobileObjects.save，其餘部分保持不變），或 Microsoft Store 版本的 MobileObjects.save。金錢、露營補給、難度、每名隊員的經驗、屬性和技能，以及你攜帶或存放在儲藏箱中的每一堆物品的數量。技能按投入其中的技能點計算。經驗足夠後，等級在遊戲中提升。其餘內容都在樹狀檢視中。請先關閉遊戲。

Game screen: money, camping supplies, the difficulty, each party member's experience, attributes, skills and unspent skill points, and the size of every stack in the inventory, stash and ingredients. The .savegame zip is opened and its object list edited in place; the rest is kept. Checked on public saves, not yet tried in the game
= 遊戲畫面：金錢、露營補給、難度、每名隊員的經驗、屬性、技能和未使用的技能點，以及物品欄、儲藏箱和材料中每一堆物品的數量。開啟 .savegame 壓縮包並原地編輯其物件清單；其餘部分保持不變。已在公開存檔上檢查，尚未在遊戲中試過

# presets, command line, portable mode, open with
Adds the program to Explorer's Open with list for file types that belong to one game family (.sl2, .rpgsave, .rmmzsave, .rxdata, .lsv, .ess, .fos, .d2s, .es3, .sii), for your account only. It never becomes the default program, and turning this off removes it again.
= 僅為您的帳戶，將本程式新增到資源管理器針對某一遊戲系列專屬檔案型別（.sl2、.rpgsave、.rmmzsave、.rxdata、.lsv、.ess、.fos、.d2s、.es3、.sii）的「開啟方式」清單中。它不會成為預設程式，關閉後會再次移除。

Carried items to 99 (RPG Maker MV / MZ)
= 將攜帶的物品提高到 99（RPG Maker MV / MZ）

= 將攜帶的武器和防具提高到 99（RPG Maker MV / MZ）

Choose a preset file someone shared with you, to look at it and apply it
= 選擇別人分享給您的預設檔案，檢視並應用

= 無法更改「開啟方式」清單：{0}

# browser version: saving in place

Before a file opened with Open to save in place is written, the file as it is then is kept here, unless it is just what this page saved there last. These copies live in this browser's own storage, not in a folder on your computer: clearing this site's data in the browser removes them. The newest {0} are kept, up to {1} MB in all.
= 在寫入用“開啟以儲存回原檔案”開啟的檔案之前，會把檔案當時的樣子儲存在這裡，除非它正是此頁面上次儲存進去的內容。這些副本位於此瀏覽器自己的儲存中，而不是你電腦上的資料夾裡：在瀏覽器中清除此網站的資料會將其刪除。最多保留最新的 {0} 份，總計不超過 {1} MB。

Couldn't hand over the kept copy: {0}
= 無法提供儲存的副本：{0}

Couldn't open the file: {0}
= 無法開啟檔案：{0}

Couldn't read {0} from your computer again: {1}. What is on screen wasn't changed.
= 無法從你的電腦重新讀取 {0}：{1}。螢幕上的內容沒有改變。

Couldn't save to {0}: {1}. The browser can't tell whether the file on your computer changed. Your changes are still here, and copies of the file kept in this browser are under Backups › Originals kept in this browser.
= 無法儲存到 {0}：{1}。瀏覽器無法判斷你電腦上的檔案是否已更改。你的更改仍在這裡，此瀏覽器中儲存的該檔案副本位於：備份 › 儲存在此瀏覽器中的原檔案。

Couldn't save to {0}: {1}. The file on your computer wasn't changed. Your changes are still here: save again, or use Save a copy as to download them.
= 無法儲存到 {0}：{1}。你電腦上的檔案未被修改。你的更改仍在這裡：請再次儲存，或用“另存副本為”下載。

Download a file as it was before you saved it in place
= 下載某個檔案在儲存回原檔案之前的樣子

If you save anyway, the file as it is now is kept in this browser first: Backups › Originals kept in this browser.
= 如果仍然儲存，會先把檔案現在的樣子儲存在此瀏覽器中：備份 › 儲存在此瀏覽器中的原檔案。

Not saved: this page no longer has access to {0}, so it wasn't touched. Your changes are still here; Save a copy as downloads them.
= 未儲存：此頁面已無法訪問 {0}，因此沒有動它。你的更改仍在這裡；“另存副本為”可以下載它們。

= 未儲存：自此頁面上次讀取或寫入以來，你電腦上的 {0} 已被更改，因此沒有動它。你的更改仍在這裡。

Nothing is kept yet.
= 還沒有儲存任何內容。

= 瀏覽器會詢問此頁面能否修改該檔案，然後寫回你開啟的檔案。除非該檔案正是此頁面上次儲存進去的內容，否則會先把它儲存在此瀏覽器中：備份 › 儲存在此瀏覽器中的原檔案。

Simulation, farming and management
= 模擬、農場與經營

Skills and personality go from 0 to 1000; every 100 is one point on the game's bars. Sims are listed by their id (their names are in the character files). Townies and the default families are under Other Sims on the Values tab with Rarely needed on. Not yet tried in the game.
= 技能和性格範圍為0到1000；每100對應遊戲條上的一點。模擬人以ID列出（其名稱在角色檔案中）。鎮民和預設家庭位於“數值”標籤頁開啟“很少用到”後的“其他模擬人”下。尚未在遊戲中測試。

Skills and personality go from 0 to 1000; every 100 is one point on the game's bars. Sims carry their file name (user00012 is Characters\\User00012.iff). Townies and visitors are under Other Sims on the Values tab with Rarely needed on. Not yet tried in the game.
= 技能和性格範圍為0到1000；每100對應遊戲條上的一點。模擬人使用其檔名（user00012即Characters\User00012.iff）。鎮民和訪客位於“數值”標籤頁開啟“很少用到”後的“其他模擬人”下。尚未在遊戲中測試。
